Reading change: the ayah carries a relational loss beneath the punishment image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_30e5424d49968889c4b3","text":"Purification in 92:18 unexpectedly activated the craft image `ص ل ي:B004` of fire-straightening. Yet 92:16’s denial and 92:17’s avoidance correct an overreading: the packet does not say exposure reforms the subject, so the branch is retained only as an exploratory image of testing or manifesting crookedness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_40695f2e97a0928d5dbf","text":"1. **Exposure as the end of a practiced hardship.** `يَصْلَىٰهَا` anchors `ص ل ي:B003` (encountering fire and suffering its heat), and `ٱلْأَشْقَى` anchors `ش ق و:B002` (hardship, strenuous suffering, and practiced endurance). `ٱلْعُسْرَىٰ` in 92:10 supplies the direct hardship analogue, while falling, warning, denial, and turning in 92:11–16 give it a progressive history. The abductive move is that the superlative names a person formed through that course rather than an arbitrary label attached at exposure. The ayah reads as convergence between a cultivated hardship and its thermal encounter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_48010fdf9d968b22f522","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:15:2\",\"root_000880\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Öncünün hemen ardından giden yarışçı boyutu, kişiyi seçtiği yolun peşinden ateş temasına kadar ilerleyen biri gibi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B001","root_000880/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5181542436fd98746278","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:15:2\",\"root_000880\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Dua ve esenlik boyutları, yönlendirilmiş ilişkiyi reddeden kişinin sonunda yalnız yakıcı tarafla karşılaşması şeklinde ters bir ilişki kurabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_71281d96ade05a6d69db","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:15:2\",\"root_000880\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ateşte çevirerek doğrultma görüntüsü, sıcaklığı eğriliği sınayan veya açığa çıkaran bir kuvvet gibi düşündürür; ayet bu temasın kişiyi düzelttiğini söylemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_94f1d0e0355789d72c4c","text":"1. **Encountering fire as exclusive extremity.** Evidence: `ص ل ي` B003 (encountering fire/heat), `ش ق و` B002 (hardship/suffering). Structural cue: the negated-except form narrows the fire’s direct encounter to the most wretched. Mechanism: the path of hardship culminates in embodied contact with heat. Abductive move: I infer that “most wretched” names the fully realized endpoint of 92:10’s العسرى. Reading change: entry into fire is not arbitrary but path-completion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a91edce6bc45704fab61","text":"92:17-18 retrospectively fused the two verbs: the one who “gave and guarded” becomes “the most guarded” who gives wealth to purify himself. The surprise is that taqwā is not only interior caution; it becomes a spatial shield completed by divine removal from fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003","root_000880/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae16a5688b5e3a941b28","text":"2. **Exploratory fire-straightening image.** `ص ل ي:B004` includes heating and turning a stick over fire to straighten it. Guidance in 92:12 and purification in 92:18 activate the neighboring ideas of direction and correction, while `لَا ... إِلَّا` sharply limits who meets this operation. The packet does not say the encounter reforms its subject; mapping the craft image onto exposure is therefore explicitly abductive, and `ص ل ي:B003` remains primary.
